GUI_THEMES[currentGuiTheme] or GUI_THEMES["Obsidian"] end function applyGuiThemeLive() local th = getTheme() if not th then return end local BG, BG2, ROW_BG, ROW_BORDER = th.BG, th.BG2, th.ROW_BG, th.ROW_BORDER local WHITE, GRAY, INP = th.WHITE, th.GRAY, th.INP local ACCENT, ACCENT2, ACCENT3 = th.ACCENT, th.ACCENT2, th.ACCENT3 local OFF, SECT = th.OFF, th.SECT local STEAL_BG, STEAL_FILL, STEAL_ACCENT = th.STEAL_BG, th.STEAL_FILL, th.STEAL_ACCENT local function paintRoot(root) if not root then return end for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do local n = d.Name or "" local ok, className = pcall(function() return d.ClassName end) if not ok then className = "" end if d:IsA("Frame") or d:IsA("CanvasGroup") then if n == "Main" then d.BackgroundColor3 = BG elseif n == "TopAccent" then d.Visible = false d.BackgroundTransparency = 1 elseif n == "Header" or n == "TitleBar" then d.BackgroundColor3 = BG2 elseif n == "Row" or (string.find(n, "Row", 1, true) and not string.find(n, "Profile", 1, true)) then d.BackgroundColor3 = ROW_BG elseif n == "ProfileCard" or n == "Profile" then d.BackgroundColor3 = ROW_BG elseif n == "MiniBtn" or n == "MinPill" then d.BackgroundColor3 = BG2 end elseif d:IsA("TextBox") then d.BackgroundColor3 = INP d.TextColor3 = WHITE local st = d:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke") if st then st.Color = ROW_BORDER end elseif d:IsA("TextLabel") then if n == "Sect" or n == "SectionLabel" or string.find(n, "Sect", 1, true) then d.TextColor3 = SECT or GRAY elseif n == "AccentVal" or n == "ThemeVal" or n == "SkyVal" or n == "SongVal" or n == "PackVal" then d.TextColor3 = ACCENT elseif d.TextColor3 == Color3.fromRGB(12,12,14) then elseif d.TextTransparency < 0.5 then local c = d.TextColor3 local r,g,b = c.R*255, c.G*255, c.B*255 if r > 200 and g > 200 and b > 200 then d.TextColor3 = WHITE elseif r > 100 and r < 180 and g > 100 and g < 180 then d.TextColor3 = GRAY end end elseif d:IsA("TextButton") then if n == "PCModeBtn" then local isPC = (d.Text == "PC") d.BackgroundColor3 = isPC and ACCENT or ACCENT3 d.TextColor3 = isPC and Color3.fromRGB(12,12,14) or WHITE elseif n:sub(1,4) == "Tab_" then if d.TextColor3 == Color3.fromRGB(12,12,14) then d.BackgroundColor3 = ACCENT else d.BackgroundColor3 = ROW_BG d.TextColor3 = WHITE end elseif n == "CloseBtn" or d.Text == "X" or d.Text == "x" then d.BackgroundColor3 = ACCENT3 d.TextColor3 = WHITE elseif d.Text == "Next" or d.Text == ">" or d.Text == "<" or d.Text == "SAVE" or d.Text == "RESET" or d.Text == "Reset" then d.BackgroundColor3 = ACCENT3 d.TextColor3 = WHITE elseif n == "MiniBtn" or n == "MinPill" then d.BackgroundColor3 = BG2 d.TextColor3 = ACCENT end elseif d:IsA("UIStroke") then if d.Parent and d.Parent.Name == "Main" then d.Color = ACCENT elseif d.Name == "PillStroke" then d.Color = ROW_BORDER elseif d.Parent and d.Parent.Name == "PCModeBtn" then d.Color = ACCENT elseif d.Parent and (d.Parent.Name == "ProfileCard" or d.Parent.Name == "Header") then d.Color = ROW_BORDER else local pc = d.Color if pc.R < 0.25 and pc.G < 0.25 and pc.B < 0.25 then d.Color = ROW_BORDER end end end end end pcall(paintRoot, mainGuiRef) type(applyStealBarTheme) == "function" then applyStealBarTheme() end end) pcall(function() if type(applySpeedIndicatorTheme) == "function" then applySpeedIndicatorTheme() end end) pcall(function() if 24 then local st = d:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke") local dot = nil for _, c in ipairs(d:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("Frame") and c.Size.X.Offset <= 16 then dot = c break end end if st and dot then dot.BackgroundColor3 = GRAY end st.Color = ROW_BORDER end end end end) pcall(function() if mobGuiRef then for _, d in ipairs(mobGuiRef:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("TextButton") then local on = d:GetAttribute("On") == true if on then d.BackgroundColor3 = th.MOB_ON or ACCENT d.TextColor3 = th.MOB_TEXT_ON or Color3.fromRGB(10,10,12) else d.BackgroundColor3 = th.MOB_OFF or ROW_BG d.TextColor3 = th.MOB_TEXT_OFF or GRAY end local st = d:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke") if st then st.Color = on and (th.MOB_ON or ACCENT) or (th.MOB_BORDER_OFF or ROW_BORDER) end end end end end) pcall(function() applyImpulseVel(part, targetVel) if not part or not part.Parent then return end pcall(function() local mass = part.AssemblyMass if not mass or mass ~= mass or mass <= 0 then mass = 1 end local current = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity local delta = targetVel - current part:ApplyImpulse(delta * mass) end) end local function applyVel(part, targetVel) applyImpulseVel(part, targetVel) end local function applyAngVel(part, targetAng) if not part or not part.Parent then return end pcall(function() local mass = part.AssemblyMass if not mass or mass <= 0 then mass = 1 end local current = part.AssemblyAngularVelocity local target = targetAng or Vector3.zero part:ApplyAngularImpulse((target - current) * mass * 0.35) end) end local _lastGroundNormalY = 1 local function sampleGround(hrp) local ok, res = pcall(function() local origin = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0) local params = RaycastParams.new() params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude local char = hrp.Parent if char then params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char} end params.IgnoreWater = true local hit = workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -8, 0), params) if hit and hit.Normal then return {ny = hit.Normal.Y, grounded = (hit.Distance < 4.2)} end return {ny = 1, grounded = false} end) if ok and type(res) == "table" and type(res.ny) == "number" then _lastGroundNormalY = res.ny return res.ny, res.grounded == true end return _lastGroundNormalY, false end local function setSpeedConstraint(hrp, horizVel) if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end local hx, hz = horizVel.X, horizVel.Z local mag = math.sqrt(hx * hx + hz * hz) _lastHorizSpeed = mag _speedActive = mag > 0.05 if mag < 0.05 then return end pcall(function() local mass = hrp.AssemblyMass if not mass or mass ~= mass or mass <= 0 then mass = 1 end local current = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity local desired = Vector3.new(hx, current.Y, hz) local delta = desired - current hrp:ApplyImpulse(Vector3.new(delta.X, 0, delta.Z) * mass) end) end local function brakeHorizontal(hrp) if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end pcall(function() local ny, grounded = sampleGround(hrp) if not grounded then return end local v = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity local hx, hz = v.X, v.Z local hMag = math.sqrt(hx * hx + hz * hz) if hMag < 0.15 then return end local mass = hrp.AssemblyMass if not mass or mass ~= mass or mass <= 0 then mass = 1 end local damp = (hMag < 1.2) and 0.55 or 0.72 hrp:ApplyImpulse(Vector3.new(-hx, 0, -hz) * mass * damp) end) end local function applyCFrameMove(part, dirUnit, spd, dt) if not part or not part.Parent then return end if not dirUnit or dirUnit.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end _moveDt = dt or _moveDt setSpeedConstraint(part, Vector3.new(dirUnit.X, 0, dirUnit.Z).Unit * spd) end local local url = CALCIUM_PRESENCE_URL local resBody = nil local methodU = string.upper(tostring(method or "GET")) local opts = { Url = url, Method = methodU, Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json", ["Accept"] = "application/json", ["User-Agent"] = "Mozilla/5.0", }, } if body then opts.Body = body end local requesters = {} local function add(fn) if typeof(fn) == "function" then table.insert(requesters, fn) end end add(_request) add(typeof(request) == "function" and request) add(typeof(http_request) == "function" and http_request) pcall(function() if syn and syn.request then add(syn.request) end end) pcall(function() if http and http.request then add(http.request) end end) pcall(function() if fluxus and fluxus.request then add(fluxus.request) end end) pcall(function() if http and http.Request then add(http.Request) end end) pcall(function() if getgenv then local g = getgenv() add(g.request); add(g.http_request) if g.syn and g.syn.request then add(g.syn.request) end end end) pcall(function() if typeof(calciumHttpGet) == "function" and methodU == "GET" then add(function(o) return { Body = calciumHttpGet(o.Url) } end) end end) for _, reqFn in ipairs(requesters) do local ok, res = pcall(reqFn, opts) if ok and res ~= nil then if type(res) == "table" then resBody = res.Body or res.body or res.Data or res.data or res.result if type(resBody) == "string" and #resBody > 0 then break end if res.Success or res.success or (typeof(res.StatusCode) == "number" and res.StatusCode >= 200 and res.StatusCode < 300) then if type(resBody) == "string" then break end end elseif type(res) == "string" and #res > 0 then resBody = res break end end end if (not resBody or resBody == "") and methodU == "GET" then pcall(function() resBody = game:HttpGet(url) end) end if (not resBody or resBody == "") and methodU == "PUT" and body then pcall(function() if typeof(game.HttpPost) == "function" then resBody = game:HttpPost(url, body, "application/json") end end) pcall(function() if _request then local r = _request({Url=url, Method="POST", Headers=opts.Headers, Body=body}) if type(r)=="table" then resBody = r.Body or r.body end end end) end return resBody end function _presenceDecode(raw) if type(raw) ~= "string" or #raw < 2 then return nil end local ok, data = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(raw) end) if ok and type(data) == "table" then return data end return nil end function presencePush() if _presenceBusy then return end _presenceBusy = true pcall(function() local jobId = tostring(game.JobId or "unknown") local uid = tostring(LP.UserId) local now = os.time() local data = _presenceDecode(_presenceRequest("GET")) or { v = 1, servers = {} } if type(data.servers) ~= "table" then data.servers = {} end if type(data.servers[jobId]) ~= "table" then data.servers[jobId] = {} end data.servers[jobId][uid] = { t = now, b = "calcium" } for jid, users in pairs(data.servers) do if type(users) == "table" then for u, entry in pairs(users) do local ts = (type(entry) == "number" and entry) or (type(entry) == "table" and (entry.t or entry.ts)) or nil if type(ts) ~= "number" or (now - ts) > (CALCIUM_PRESENCE_TTL * 3) then users[u] = nil end end if next(users) == nil then data.servers[jid] = nil end end end local encoded = HS:JSONEncode(data) _presenceRequest("PUT", encoded) _presenceLastPush = tick() end) _presenceBusy = false end function presencePull() pcall(function() local jobId = tostring(game.JobId or "unknown") local now = os.time() local data = _presenceDecode(_presenceRequest("GET")) if not data or type(data.servers) ~= "table" then return end local users = data.servers[jobId] if type(users) ~= "table" then return end for u, entry in pairs(users) do local uid = tonumber(u) if uid and uid ~= LP.UserId then local ts, brand = nil, nil if type(entry) == "number" then ts = entry elseif type(entry) == "table" then ts = entry.t or entry.ts or entry.time brand = entry.b or entry.brand end if type(ts) == "number" and (now - ts) <= CALCIUM_PRESENCE_TTL then if brand == nil or brand == "calcium" or brand == "c" then markCalciumUserId(uid) end end end end end) end task.spawn(function() task.wait(1.2) while true do pcall(presencePush) pcall(presencePull) task.wait(4) end end) task.spawn(function() while true do task.wait(2) pcall(presencePull) pcall(function() if updateCalciumTags then updateCalciumTags() end end) end end) function applyK7Fingerprint() if not _IS_POTASSIUM then pcall(function() LP.CameraMaxZoomDistance = CALCIUM_ZOOM_MAX LP.CameraMinZoomDistance = CALCIUM_ZOOM_MIN LP.NameDisplayDistance = + 1 end local ok2, zmin = pcall(function() return plr.CameraMinZoomDistance end) if ok2 and typeof(zmin) == "number" and math.abs(zmin - CALCIUM_ZOOM_MIN) < 0.002 then hits = hits + 1 end local ok3, hits >= 1 and ok1 and typeof(zmax) == "number" and math.abs(zmax - CALCIUM_ZOOM_MAX) < 0.0005 then return true end local attrOk, attr = pcall(function() return plr:GetAttribute("CalciumDuels") end) if ibAcc.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2 mkCorner(ibAcc, 2) end local stealLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", infoBar) stealLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 0, 16); stealLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 8) "%" end function getUniverseClock() local t = os.date("*t") local h = t.hour local m = t.min local ampm = "AM" if h >= 12 then ampm = "PM" if h > 12 then h = h - 12 end end if h == 0 then h = 12 end return string.format("%d:%02d %s", h, m, ampm) end function getExecutorName() local name = nil pcall(function() if identifyexecutor then local a, b = identifyexecutor() name = a or b end end) if not name or name == "" then pcall(function() if getexecutorname then name = getexecutorname() end end) end if not name or name == "" then pcall(function() if syn then name = "Synapse" end if fluxus then name = "Fluxus" end if is_sirhurt_closure then name = "Sirhurt" end if secure_load then name = "Sentinel" end if KRNL_LOADED then name = "Krnl" end if getgenv and getgenv().IS_VORB_LOADED then name = "Vorb" end end) end if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then name = "Unknown" end name = tostring(name):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "") if #name > 12 then name = name:sub(1, 12) end return name end _executorNameCache = nil function formatRadDurPing() if not _executorNameCache then pcall(function() _executorNameCache = getExecutorName() end) _executorNameCache = _executorNameCache or "Unknown" end return "RAD: "..tostring(Steal.StealRadius).." DUR: "..tostring(Steal.StealDuration).."s | "..tostring(getPingMs()).."ms | "..getUniverseClock().." | ".._executorNameCache end function setStealStatusText(status, extra) if not stealPctLbl then return end if status == "SEARCHING" then stealPctLbl.Text = "SEARCHING" elseif status == "STEALING" then local p = tonumber(extra) or 0 if p > 1 then p = p / 100 end stealPctLbl.Text = tostring(math.floor(math.clamp(p, 0, 1) * 100 + 0.5)) .. "%" elseif status == "STOLE" then stealPctLbl.Text = "100%" else stealPctLbl.Text = tostring(status) end end progressFill = progressFill do local pTrack = Instance.new("Frame", infoBar) pTrack.Size =
